```
### 你补的证据（N 句）
（说明这句是人写的，不是模型写的）
- C3 · 数字指标：<claim text>
  > 你补的：「<text>」
```

Keep it tight:
```
function supplementLines(view: SessionView): string[] {
  if (view.supplements.length === 0) return [];
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('### 你补的证据（' + String(view.supplements.length) + ' 句）');
  out.push('');
  out.push('这几句是你自己写的事实，不是模型生成的；简历原文没有被改动，采纳后会作为新的一行加在对应主张那一行下面。');
  out.push('');
  for (const row of view.supplements) {
    const claim = view.claims.find((item) => item.id === row.claimId);
    out.push('- ' + row.claimId + (claim === undefined ? '' : ' · ' + claim.text));
    out.push('  > 你补的：' + row.text);
  }
  out.push('');
  return out;
}
```
Need `claimTextOf`? page: report.ts has claimTextOf defined for requirements? It said report.ts has claimTextOf. Fine, use view.claims.find.

report-html.ts similarly `supplementSection(view)`.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

The v6 test creates a minimal v6 DB by hand with only session/claim/probe/event/llm_call tables — no revision table. Real v6 DBs always have revision (v5 added it). But the hand-made fixture lacks it. Options:
(a) make migrateV8 tolerant: `if (tableExists(db,'revision'))` … but a real v6 DB always has it. The fixture is an approximation.
(b) Update the fixture to include revision (and revised_resume) tables.

The fixture is meant to represent a real old DB; but the existing test was written to test v7 migration from a v6 minimal DB. Now v8 also runs, so the fixture needs revision table. Cleanest: make migrateV8 defensive? No — silently skipping a needed rebuild would be worse (the "no such table" error here is real feedback). Better: add the revision table to the fixture (it's a genuine part of v5+), which is honest.

But careful: the fixture DB at v6 would in reality also have revised_resume, interviewer, jd... The test creates a subset. Adding just the revision table (the v5 shape) is enough for v8.

Let me look at store.test.ts around lines 230-290 to patch the fixture.

</details>

2. Add interface SessionSupplement before/after SessionView. I'll add right before `export interface SessionView {`:
```
/** 用户当场补的一句真话：它不是模型生成的，也不回写简历原文。 */
export interface SessionSupplement {
  claimId: string;
  text: string;
  digest: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
```
3. Add functions after discardRevisions:
```
/** 记下一句补充说明（按主张 id 一条，重复调用就是改这一条）。 */
export async function saveSupplement(id: string, claimId: string, text: string): Promise<SessionSupplement> {
  const data = await postJson<{ supplement: SessionSupplement }>('/api/sessions/' + encodeURIComponent(id) + '/supplements', {
    claimId: claimId,
    text: text,
  });
  return data.supplement;
}

export async function deleteSupplement(id: string, claimId: string): Promise<void> {
  const res = await fetch('/api/sessions/' + encodeURIComponent(id) + '/supplements/' + encodeURIComponent(claimId), { method: 'DELETE' });
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(await readError(res), res.status);
}
```
Need to check `postJson` and `ApiError`/`readError` exist (used above). Yes discardRevisions uses them.

Also `SessionView.supplements` — server always returns it (new code) but for safety in App: `view.supplements ?? []`.

Let me write the components.

SupplementBlock:

```tsx
/**
 * 补一句真话。改写建议是模型写的，这一句是**用户自己写的**——
 * 两者都只在点「确认采纳」之后才会进简历，所以共用同一个勾选动作。
 */
function SupplementBlock(props: {
  claimId: string;
  text: string | null;
  note?: string | null;
  onSave?: (claimId: string, text: string) => void;
  onDelete?: (claimId: string) => void;
}) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(props.text ?? '');
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  const has = props.text !== null;
  const open = !has || editing;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-mint-400/25 bg-mint-400/5 p-2.5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Badge tone="mint">补一句真话</Badge>
        <span className="text-[11px] text-ink-400">
          {has ? '这句是你写的，采纳之后会作为新的一行加进简历。' : '简历里没写、但你确实做过的事，写在这里；它会变成简历里新的一行。'}
        </span>
        {has ? (
          <button type="button" data-shot={'supplement-edit-' + props.claimId} className="ml-auto ..." onClick={() => { setDraft(props.text ?? ''); setEditing(true); }}>改一句</button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      {has && !editing ? (
        <p data-shot={'supplement-text-' + props.claimId} className="text-[12.5px] text-mint-300">{props.text}</p>
      ) : null}
      {open ? (
        <>
          <textarea data-shot={'supplement-input-' + props.claimId} value={draft} rows={2} placeholder="例如：日订单 30 万，峰值 QPS 5000。" className="..." onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)} />
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <button type="button" data-shot={'supplement-save-' + props.claimId} className="..." onClick={() => props.onSave?.(props.claimId, draft)}>存下这句</button>
            {has ? <button data-shot={'supplement-delete-' + props.claimId} onClick={() => props.onDelete?.(props.claimId)}>删掉</button> : null}
            {editing ? <button onClick={() => setEditing(false)}>收起</button> : null}
          </div>
        </>
      ) : null}
      {props.note == null ? null : <p data-shot={'supplement-note-' + props.claimId} className="text-[11.5px] text-amber-ink">{props.note}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

The failing case X2 (`start 3, end 3, before ''`) is now a legal insertion — that's exactly the intended new semantics. But careful: `lastEnd = end` = 3 for insertion; and `start < lastEnd` check means a later revision with start < 3 is rejected. Since insertions have zero width, `lastEnd` should probably stay as the previous value for insertions so that another revision starting at the same point... Hmm, insertion at position 3 and then a replacement starting at 3: sorted order puts zero-width insertion first (start 3 = start 3, tie by claimId). Then replacement start 3 < lastEnd 3 → rejected as overlapping. Is that desirable? Trace: an insertion at position 3 and a replacement at 3-8 are different pieces of text (insertion inserts a newline before the line, replacement changes words at that position). Arguably both should be allowed since they don't overlap in the source range (empty range). Better: `lastEnd = end` only when not insertion; for insertion don't advance lastEnd (leave as is). Actually insertion at 3 after replacement ending at 3: lastEnd=3, insertion start 3, not < 3, allowed. Good. And insertion at 3 followed by replacement 3-8: if we don't touch lastEnd for insertion, lastEnd stays -1 → replacement allowed. But then the delta computation: text insertion changed length by after.length; positions shift by delta, and the replacement at source 3 maps to 3+delta — correct, since insertion went at at=3+delta(0)=3 and then delta becomes after.length; the replacement at source 3 maps to 3 + after.length which is right after the inserted text? Wait: insertion at position 3 inserts before the character at index 3; the replacement covers source [3,8) which now lives at [3+len(after), 8+len(after)). text.slice(0, at) + after + text.slice(at + 0) → inserted at 3; then replacement at 3+len → correct.

So for correctness, insertions shouldn't advance lastEnd. Let me check: for two insertions at the same point with different claimIds — ordered by claimId, first one applied, lastEnd unchanged (-1), second also applied at same source position, delta already grew, so at = 3 + len(first) → both inserted in order. Fine.

Also X3 (`start 0, end 4, before = RESUME.slice(0,4), after ''`) — now rejected by `after.length === 0` → '改写后的内容是空的'. Good, still skipped.

So fix: in the applied push, set `lastEnd = end` only if not insertion. Also the test must be updated: case X2 is no longer a rejection; replace with a case that should still be rejected, e.g. `{ claimId: 'X2', start: 3, end: 3, before: 'x', after: 'y' }` — zero width but before non-empty → insertion false → end <= start → rejected. Or add a new test asserting insertion is legal.

Also, careful: `sameText(resumeText.slice(3,3), '')` → '' vs '' → true (actual === claimed) so insertion passes.

Plan:
1. revise.ts: change `lastEnd = end;` to insertion-aware:
```
    // 插入型不推进最右边界：它没有覆盖原文，后面的改写不算与它重叠
    if (!insertion) lastEnd = end;
```
2. revise.test.ts: update the failing test: change X2 to `{ claimId: 'X2', start: 3, end: 3, before: 'x', after: 'y' }` and title mention, and add a new test「插入型区间合法：它不覆盖原文，也不挡后面的改写」 asserting applied length 2 with start===end && before==='' and the replacement still applied.

Let me check the test file around to add new test after :66. Also there might already be an insertion test in supplement.test.ts (yes, 「应用后多一行且原文不动」). Additional test for lastEnd behavior is worthwhile — it covers the interleaving rule.

Let me first read the region of revise.test.ts lines 1-30 to know RESUME and imports for writing the new test.

```text
4| * 界面要回答三个问题，所以这里的函数也按这三件事组织：
5| * 1. 我勾了哪几条？（勾选状态只是本地数组，服务端每次都从原文重算）
6| * 2. 现在改到哪一步了？（已采纳 / 还没定 / 改不动，三个数分别数）
7| * 3. 这次改动了多少？（改完的简历比原文长还是短，用户得看得见）
8| *
9| * 与 packages/server/src/revisions.ts 是两份代码、同一套语义：前端不能 import shared
10| * （node:crypto 会打进浏览器包），所以类型在这里重复声明一份，靠测试对账。
11| */
12|
13|/** 一条落地的改写：区间是**原文**坐标，before 是原文该区间的逐字内容 */
14|export interface RevisionItem {
15|  claimId: string;
16|  start: number;
17|  end: number;
18|  before: string;
19|  after: string;
20|}
21|
22|/** 被跳过的建议与理由（人话，直接显示） */
23|export interface RevisionSkipItem {
24|  claimId: string;
25|  reason: string;
26|}
27|
28|/** 服务端返回的那一版改完的简历 */
29|export interface RevisedResume {
30|  text: string;
31|  digest: string;
32|  /** 已落地的改写，原文坐标 */
33|  applied: RevisionItem[];
34|  /** 同上，但是**新文本**坐标：给高亮用 */
35|  spans: RevisionItem[];
36|  accepted: string[];
37|  pending: string[];
38|  skipped: RevisionSkipItem[];
39|  updatedAt: string;
40|}
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Great progress — all new assertions pass up through the export. Failure: after clicking delete, the note never shows '已删掉'.

Why? In ClaimBoard, the delete button only renders when `open` is true... Let's check SupplementBlock logic: after saving, `has = true`, `editing` false → open = false → so the textarea/buttons (including delete) are hidden! So `clickShot('supplement-delete-...')` clicked nothing (returned 'missing' but we ignore), and the note stayed as the earlier saved note ('已记下...').

So to delete, the user must first click 「改一句」 (supplement-edit-<id>) to open. That's the designed flow. Fix the e2e: click `supplement-edit-<id>` first, then delete.

Also the `hasShot`/`clickShot` returning 'missing' should maybe be asserted. Let's add a check: after save, delete button is hidden until 「改一句」 is clicked → that's a real UI behavior worth asserting.

Update the e2e cleanup section:
```js
const deleteHidden = await hasShot('supplement-delete-' + supId);
check('存下之后按钮收起，要改或要删得先点「改一句」', deleteHidden === 'no', deleteHidden);
await clickShot('supplement-edit-' + supId);
const reopened = await hasShot('supplement-delete-' + supId);
check('点「改一句」之后能删掉这句', reopened === 'yes', reopened);
await clickShot('supplement-delete-' + supId);
```

Now the splicer script.

splicer: 
```js
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
const ROOT = 'D:/CZB';
const target = ROOT + '/tools/ui-shots/shots.mjs';
const helper = readFileSync(ROOT + '/.tmp/shots-helper.txt', 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n+$/, '');
const shot = readFileSync(ROOT + '/.tmp/shots-shot.txt', 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n+$/, '');
const lines = readFileSync(target, 'utf8').split('\n');
let helperAt = -1, shotAt = -1;
for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  if (helperAt < 0 && lines[i].indexOf('const click = (shot) =>') >= 0) helperAt = i;
  if (lines[i].indexOf("'撤销结论', 20000);") >= 0) shotAt = i;
}
if (helperAt < 0) throw new Error('helper anchor not found');
if (shotAt < 0) throw new Error('shot anchor not found');
const out = lines.slice();
out.splice(shotAt + 1, 0, ...shot.split('\n'));
out.splice(helperAt + 1, 0, ...helper.split('\n'));
writeFileSync(target, out.join('\n'));
console.log('helper@' + String(helperAt + 1) + ' shot@' + String(shotAt + 1) + ' lines ' + String(lines.length) + ' -> ' + String(out.length));
```
Note: inserting shot first at shotAt+1 then helper at helperAt+1 — helperAt < shotAt so indices unaffected (insertion after helper shifts shot anchor by helper lines, but we already computed shotAt index in old array; splicing into `out` at helperAt+1 first shifts shotAt... order matters: I splice shot first (at shotAt+1) then helper (at helperAt+1) — helperAt is before shotAt, and inserting at shotAt+1 doesn't change indices ≤ shotAt. So helperAt still valid. Good.

Check: '撤销结论', 20000); appears once? Earlier grep showed line 441 exactly. Should be unique. Let me verify with grep first, and also verify 'const click = (shot) =>' unique.

Now the splicer script. Write it via tools.write (content lines with escaped single quotes as needed).

splicer content:
```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// 把两段待插入的代码按锚点拼进 tools/ui-shots/shots.mjs（只做一次，插过就别再跑）。
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';

const ROOT = 'D:/CZB';
const target = ROOT + '/tools/ui-shots/shots.mjs';
const helper = readFileSync(ROOT + '/.tmp/shots-helper.txt', 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n+$/, '');
const shot = readFileSync(ROOT + '/.tmp/shots-shot.txt', 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n+$/, '');
const source = readFileSync(target, 'utf8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
if (source.indexOf('const setField = (shot, text)') >= 0) throw new Error('已经插过了，先 git checkout -- tools/ui-shots/shots.mjs');
const lines = source.split('\n');
let helperAt = -1;
let shotAt = -1;
for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
  if (helperAt < 0 && lines[i].indexOf('const click = (shot) =>') >= 0) helperAt = i;
  if (lines[i].indexOf("'撤销结论', 20000);") >= 0) shotAt = i;
}
if (helperAt < 0) throw new Error('找不到 click 这一行的锚点');
if (shotAt < 0) throw new Error('找不到撤销结论那一行的锚点');
if (shotAt < helperAt) throw new Error('锚点顺序不对');
const out = lines.slice();
out.splice(shotAt + 1, 0, ...shot.split('\n'));
out.splice(helperAt + 1, 0, ...helper.split('\n'));
writeFileSync(target, out.join('\n'));
console.log('helper @' + String(helperAt + 1) + '，shot @' + String(shotAt + 1) + '，' + String(lines.length) + ' 行 -> ' + String(out.length) + ' 行');
```

Note: `'撤销结论', 20000);` inside a double-quoted JS string in the splicer file — fine.

Write with tools.write using joined single-quoted lines with escapes.

No! `querySelector("supplement-input-C1")` is a *type selector* (element name), not an attribute selector. I dropped the `[data-shot=...]` wrapper!

In my "clean" rewrite I wrote `document.querySelector(" + JSON.stringify(shot) + ")` — missing the surrounding `[data-shot=...]`. That's the bug. Must be:

```
    const setField = (shot, text) => "(() => { const el = document.querySelector('[data-shot=\"' + shot + '\"]'); ...
```
Using JSON.stringify for the attribute selector value:
```
const setField = (shot, text) => "(() => { const el = document.querySelector(" + JSON.stringify('[data-shot="' + shot + '"]') + "); ...";
```
JSON.stringify of the full selector string gives `"[data-shot=\"supplement-input-C1\"]"` — a valid JS string literal with escaped double quotes inside → page expression: `document.querySelector("[data-shot=\"supplement-input-C1\"]")` ✓ valid.

That's clean and avoids quoting pain. Let me fix the helper line accordingly. Same for other selectors? The other page expressions I wrote as `document.querySelector(" + supNoteSel + ")` where supNoteSel = JSON.stringify('supplement-note-C1') → also type-selector bug! Design sketch for A4 反问环节 (candidate asks the interviewer back):
- After verdicts (the panel has judged the claims), the candidate gets to ask the panel N questions back (reverse questions). Purpose: simulate the "你有什么想问我们的吗" part of a real interview, and check whether the candidate's questions show understanding of the role.
- But wait: does this serve the judging criteria? It reuses the pipeline (a counter stage: role='candidate' asks, interviewer answers). Value: 面试最后环节 + 用户可以在演练里练反问。

New stage `counter`:
- Input: 目标岗位 JD + 已被判定站不住的主张（或整个 session 摘要）→ 模型生成 3 条「求职者该问的问题」（带意图标签：问团队/问技术栈/问成长/问业务），每条 rationale「为什么这个问题值得问」。
- Then 面试官回答（role of 面试官）？That doubles cassettes. Keep to one call: 生成 3 条反问 + 面试官的一句回应（回答 + 一个反问回敬「这个问题说明你更在意什么」）. Hmm, that's a single LLM call per session → +1 cassette. Good.

Data:
- `CounterQuestion { id, text, kind: 'team'|'tech'|'growth'|'business'|'process', reason, answer, followUp }`? Keep narrow: { id, text, kind, reason, reply }.
- shared: `COUNTER_KIND_LABEL`, `counterKind` enum, schema `counterListSchema` (max 4?), `counterQuestions()` builder? Keep: schema + labels + a pure function to pick which ones to show.
- server: orchestrator/counter.ts: `runCounter({client, claims, verdicts, jd, requirements})` → { questions, notices, usage }. Stage 'counter' in prompts.ts with COUNTER_SYSTEM + buildCounterPrompt.
- run.ts: after suggest, if budget/session ok → emit stage 'counter' → emit event {type:'counters', counters}.
- store: new table `counter` (v9) or reuse event + a JSON column in session meta? Per F54 (落库纪律): if shown in UI, must land in business table → new table `counter` with SCHEMA_VERSION 9 → but careful: schema v9 migration (add table only → simple migration).
- pipeline: SessionView.counters; report section 「五、反问环节」? Simpler: append to report as a section; UI: a pane block? UI is already 5 columns... Add to StageRail (过程面板) a 「轮到你问了」 block after shortfall, and to the report.

Hmm. Given effort, maybe simpler: A4 as a **report + 过程面板** feature (no new column). That is honest: "轮到你问了：3 条建议提问（问团队/问技术栈/问成长）+ 面试官可能会怎么接".

Cassette: +1 replay cassette (32 → 33) and must re-record (seed:demo) because prompts unchanged? Adding a new stage doesn't invalidate existing cassettes (fingerprints unaffected as long as existing prompts unchanged). Existing prompts: extract/probe/answer/verdict/suggest untouched → those cassettes stay. New stage adds 1 new cassette. But 'counter' purpose requires the same shape; the digest only covers system/messages, so new stage = new digests, no impact on old. Good: 32 → 33 盘.

But wait: scripted-demo must synthesize the counter response for offline demo (F56 lesson: 离线演示的覆盖面 = 演示脚本的覆盖面). And demo-seed's `required` list must include 'counter'.

Also the run flow order: counter after suggest (both after verdicts). Since counter summarizes verdicts, it needs claim verdicts — available.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I need to design A4 concretely. Let me think about what 反问环节 (counter-questions) means and how it best serves the competition.

Real interview ending: "你有什么想问我的吗？" The candidate asks 2–3 questions. Value for the user:
1. 演练这个环节（很多人答不好）。
2. 反向检查：候选人的问题暴露他/她对岗位的理解 —— 也照出 JD 与简历的缺口。
3. 由面试官给出一句回应 + 一个「你更在意什么」的观察。

Implementation (single LLM call, stage `counter`):
- Input: JD（原文 + 抽出的要求，若有）+ 判定结果摘要（每条主张的判定 kind）+ 建议摘要? Keep: claims + verdicts + requirements.
- Output (zod): `{ questions: [{ text, kind, reason, reply }] }` where:
  - text: 求职者该问的问题（1–200）
  - kind: enum team|tech|growth|business|process
  - reason: 为什么这个问题在这场面里值得问（1–200），并且引用一条要求或一条主张 —— 锚定：可以要求 reason 携带 `quote` 指向 JD 原文或简历原文？That would be strong (双锚点再次复用) but adds complexity. Actually a nice extension of 证据锚定: the counter question must be anchored to either a JD requirement or a claim. Hmm, "为什么值得问" quoting the JD requirement is genuinely useful.
  
  Let me keep it: `{ text, kind, reason, quote, source: 'jd'|'resume' }` — quote must resolve against JD text or resume text; drop those that can't anchor (consistent with the project's core rule: 无锚点不许进系统). For a session without JD, source must be 'resume' (anchor to a claim's quoted text). The reason explains.

  Wait, that makes counter questions require anchors → resolveAnchor on jd.text or resumeText. Feasible: pass both. Use `resolveAnchor(source, {quote})`.

- reply: 面试官的一句回应（1–200）—— 让 scene 完整（"这个问题问到了点子上/这个我们内部还在试"）。

Then:
- shared: `COUNTER_KIND_LABEL`, `CounterQuestion` type, `counterDraftSchema`/`counterListSchema`, pure fn `groundCounters({resumeText, jdText, drafts})` → { questions: CounterQuestion[], rejected } with max 4; plus `CounterQuestion { id, ordinal, text, kind, reason, reply, anchor: Anchor|null, source:'jd'|'resume' }`.

Scope check (AGENTS.md 7.3): reuses Claim/Anchor/Probe/Budget/Contract/Replay ✓; explainable in 5–8 min demo? It's a small stage, adds one screen block ✓; doesn't dilute evidence (it adds 1 cassette and a section) ✓.

Hmm — but wait. Is there a cheaper, higher-value framing? The user's P10 approval included A4 as "反问环节". Let me consider: maybe a better A4 is 「反问环节」 = 求职者提问 + 面试官回应 + 一句「这个问题暴露了你没读到 JD 里的 X」. That's what I sketched. Good.

Simplification to keep cassettes at 1: one call producing all 3 questions + replies.

Decision: implement as designed.

Now data flow details:
- run.ts: after suggestions, `if (claims.length > 0)` → emit stage 'counter' → runCounter({client, claims, verdicts, jdText, requirements, resumeText}) → emit event { type:'counters', questions, rejected }.
- RunSessionResult gains `counters: CounterQuestion[]`, `counterRejected: RejectedCounter[]`.
- store: table `counter` (session_id, id, ordinal, text, kind, reason, reply, quote, start, end, exact, source, created_at) → SCHEMA_VERSION 9 + migrateV9 (add table only).

Let me look at STAGE_STATUS in pipeline.ts: jd-* mapped to extracting; verdict/suggest → scoring presumably. counter fits 'scoring' (it's after judging). Let me check.

- web: state.ts applyEvent 'counters' → SandboxState.counters; StageRail block data-shot="counters" with 3 cards (kind label + question + reason + reply); api.ts SessionView.counters.
- scripted-demo: counterText(prompt) deterministic 3 questions with quotes taken from the prompt (must anchor! use DEMO_JD/DEMO_RESUME substrings).
- demo-seed: required += 'counter'.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I'll append the counter domain types to domain.ts, and write counters.ts, and add schemas.

Design decisions for A4 concretely:

`CounterKind = 'team' | 'tech' | 'growth' | 'business' | 'process'`
`COUNTER_KIND_LABEL: Record<CounterKind,string> = { team:'团队与协作', tech:'技术栈与工具', growth:'成长与考核', business:'业务与产品', process:'流程与节奏' }`

Wait, "反问环节" = candidate asks questions. Kinds: 问团队 team / 问技术栈 tech / 问成长 growth / 问业务 business / 问流程 process. Labels: '团队与协作' / '技术栈与工具' / '成长与考核' / '业务与产品' / '流程与节奏'.

`CounterSource = 'jd' | 'resume'`
`COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL = { jd: '问岗位', resume: '问简历' }` — hmm. Maybe: 'jd': '依据岗位要求', 'resume': '依据简历原话'.

`CounterQuestion { id, ordinal, text, kind, reason, reply, anchor: Anchor | null, source: CounterSource }`

Wait — should `anchor` be nullable? Design said "锚不上的当场丢弃并记中文理由". If all counters must be anchored, then anchor is non-null... but what if there's no JD and no claims? Then `source` must be resume and anchor on a claim's anchor. If claims exist, we always have resume anchors. If there are no claims at all (empty extract), then we can't ground anything → all rejected. Hmm, but a counter question might legitimately be about the role generally without anchoring. However project doctrine = no anchor → not in the system. But that doctrine was for 提问 (probes). For counters, the value is "you get to ask them" — the grounding rule: reason must point to something in the JD or resume.

I'll require anchor non-null (grounded), consistent with the doctrine, and record rejected ones with reasons. If neither JD nor claims exist → counters list empty + notice "没有可依据的材料，这次没有生成反问". That keeps the rule crisp and testable.

Actually let me reconsider: a real "你有什么想问我们的吗" question is often about process (e.g. "团队的发布节奏是怎样的？"). Grounding it means the model must quote the JD/resume snippet that motivates it. With a JD present, plausible. Without JD but with claims, it can ground on a claim (e.g. "你说主导了订单重构，那这个团队有几个人？" — that's more of a probe...). Hmm, for resumes the counter is "关于你在简历里写的 X，我想反过来问一下团队这边的分工" — a bit awkward but acceptable.

I'll keep it: anchor is `Anchor | null` in the persisted type (so read-back from DB is honest about nullable rows), but `groundCounters` drops unanchored ones. Hmm — if we always drop, anchor would never be null. Simpler: make `anchor: Anchor` non-null (like Requirement) and drop unanchored. That keeps the type honest: everything stored is anchored. Let me do that — mirrors `Requirement`.

But `source` indicates which source text the anchor points into ('jd' | 'resume'), and for 'jd' which JD id. Need `jdId: string | null` so we can locate the JD text for highlighting. Let me include `jdId: string | null`.

So:
```ts
export interface CounterQuestion {
  id: string;          // Q1..Qn
  ordinal: number;
  text: string;        // 求职者要问的那句话
  kind: CounterKind;
  reason: string;      // 为什么该问这个（依据哪句材料）
  reply: string;       // 面试官/用人方的回答
  anchor: Anchor;      // reason 的依据在原文字符区间
  source: CounterSource;
  jdId: string | null; // source==='jd' 时是那份 JD 的 id
}
export interface RejectedCounter {
  text: string;
  reason: string;
}
```

Hmm, the draft from the LLM needs: text, kind, reason, quote (the anchor quote), sourceId? Design: model picks `sourceId` = either a JD id (J1) or a claim id (C1) — that's how it points at the material. Then code resolves the anchor: if sourceId starts with 'C' → resolveAnchor(resumeText, {quote}) and source='resume'; if starts with 'J' → resolveAnchor(jd.text, {quote}) and source='jd'. That's clean and mirrors probe's requirementId approach.

Draft schema:
```ts
counterDraftSchema = z.object({
  text: z.string().min(4).max(200),
  kind: z.enum(COUNTER_KIND_VALUES),
  reason: z.string().min(4).max(300),
  quote: z.string().min(4).max(400),
```

domain additions:
```
/**
 * 反问环节：一场质询结束后，轮到求职者提问。
 *
 * 五类取向不是随便分的：现场真正问得出价值的反问，基本都落在「团队 / 技术 / 成长 / 业务 / 流程」这五件事上。
 * 分类的意义在报告里——用户能看到自己的反问是不是全挤在「福利待遇」这一件事上。
 */
export type CounterKind = 'team' | 'tech' | 'growth' | 'business' | 'process';

export const COUNTER_KIND_LABEL: Record<CounterKind, string> = {
  team: '团队与协作',
  tech: '技术栈与工具',
  growth: '成长与考核',
  business: '业务与产品',
  process: '流程与节奏',
};

/**
 * 这句反问的依据出自哪里。
 * jd = 岗位要求和职责里的某句话；resume = 简历里的某条主张。
 * 没有材料就没有依据，也就不会有反问——这条规矩和追问一样硬。
 */
export type CounterSource = 'jd' | 'resume';

export const COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL: Record<CounterSource, string> = {
  jd: '依据岗位要求',
  resume: '依据简历原话',
};

export interface CounterQuestion {
  id: string;
  ordinal: number;
  /** 求职者要问出口的那句话 */
  text: string;
  kind: CounterKind;
  /** 为什么该问这个——报告里给用户看的「这句问得有依据」 */
  reason: string;
  /** 用人方大概会怎么答（模拟出来的，不是真的 HR 在答） */
  reply: string;
  source: CounterSource;
  /** 依据所在的原文区间：source=jd 时锚在那份 JD 上，resume 时锚在简历上 */
  anchor: Anchor;
  /** source=jd 时，依据出自哪份 JD */
  jdId: string | null;
}

/** 锚不到材料的反问：和 rejectedClaims 一样，是可以展示的产物，不是日志 */
export interface RejectedCounter {
  text: string;
  reason: string;
}
```

counters.ts:
```
/**
 * 反问环节：把「你有什么想问我们的吗」也做成有依据的产物。
 *
 * 三条硬规矩（与追问同源）：
 * 1. **没有材料就没有反问**。每一句反问都要指出依据出自哪份 JD 的哪句话，
 *    或者简历里的哪条主张；指不出来的当场丢弃并记中文理由。
 * 2. id 按模型返回顺序编 Q1..Qn，丢弃的条目不吃号——回放要求同样输入同样 id。
 * 3. 条数上限小（默认 6）：反问是「你想了解什么」，不是再问一遍简历。
 */

import type { Claim, CounterKind, CounterQuestion, CounterSource, JobDescription, RejectedCounter } from './domain.ts';
import { COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL } from './domain.ts';
import { resolveAnchor } from './anchor.ts';

export const COUNTER_LIMITS = {
  minChars: 4,
  maxChars: 200,
  maxReasonChars: 300,
  maxReplyChars: 400,
  maxCounters: 6,
  maxDrafts: 10,
} as const;

export const COUNTER_KIND_ORDER: readonly CounterKind[] = ['team', 'tech', 'growth', 'business', 'process'];

export interface RawCounterDraft {
  text: string;
  kind: CounterKind;
  reason: string;
  reply: string;
  quote: string;
  /** 依据出自哪儿：简历主张编号（C1）或 JD 编号（J1） */
  sourceId: string;
}

export interface GroundCountersResult {
  counters: CounterQuestion[];
  rejected: RejectedCounter[];
  notices: string[];
}

export function counterId(index: number): string {
  return 'Q' + String(index + 1);
}

export function groundCounters(input: {
  resumeText: string;
  jds: readonly JobDescription[];
  claims: readonly Claim[];
  drafts: readonly RawCounterDraft[];
  maxCounters?: number;
  minQuoteLength?: number;
}): GroundCountersResult {
  ...
}
```

groundCounters logic:
```
const maxCounters = input.maxCounters ?? COUNTER_LIMITS.maxCounters;
const minQuoteLength = input.minQuoteLength ?? 4;
const claimIds = new Set(input.claims.map((c) => c.id));
const jdById = new Map(input.jds.map((jd) => [jd.id, jd]));
const counters: CounterQuestion[] = [];
const rejected: RejectedCounter[] = [];
const notices: string[] = [];
const seen = new Set<string>();

for (const draft of input.drafts) {
  const text = draft.text.trim();
  if (text.length < COUNTER_LIMITS.minChars) { rejected.push({text, reason: '这句话太短，不构成一句反问'}); continue; }
  const dedupeKey = text; // 或者 quote+text
  if (seen.has(dedupeKey)) continue;
  const sourceId = draft.sourceId.trim();
  let source: CounterSource;
  let sourceText: string;
  let jdId: string | null = null;
  const jd = jdById.get(sourceId);
  if (jd !== undefined) { source = 'jd'; sourceText = jd.text; jdId = jd.id; }
  else if (claimIds.has(sourceId)) { source = 'resume'; sourceText = input.resumeText; }
  else { rejected.push({ text, reason: '这句反问指的依据（' + sourceId + '）不在这场材料里' }); continue; }
  const anchored = resolveAnchor(sourceText, { quote: draft.quote }, minQuoteLength);
  if (!anchored.ok) { rejected.push({ text, reason: COUNTER_SOURCE_LABEL[source] + '里找不到这句话，锚不上就当没有依据：「' + draft.quote + '」' }); continue; }
  if (counters.length >= maxCounters) { notices.push('反问超过 ' + String(maxCounters) + ' 条，后面 ' + ... ); break; }
  seen.add(dedupeKey);
  counters.push({ id: counterId(counters.length), ordinal: counters.length + 1, text, kind: draft.kind, reason: draft.reason.trim().slice(0, 300), reply: draft.reply.trim().slice(0, 400), source, anchor: anchored.anchor, jdId });
}
```
